Sideways = after a run up or down, price chops inside a range; it ends only two ways: coiling (the prior direction continues) or reversal (topping / bottoming). Set the prior from the previous wave first; a fake move at the edge (piercing the edge on heavy volume, then reclaiming it) flips the call against the failed direction (naughty boss 2026-10-29). Backtest (362 tickers, 14 years): none of the four readings can tell which way the range breaks; descriptive only, no position sizing.

Shape = chart / ratings / expectations (what everyone sees); Structure = floor / volume / dealer / far-month anchor / take-delivery date (what the money is doing); opposed = a setup, aligned = a trend (naughty boss 2026-10-28; method not backtested, unverified).

Breathing

Backtest (2026-10-07): putting the strike at the inhale top is about the same as selling 4-6% out of the money, by the rule pierced 33%, portfolio -1.75% per year (other tickers), unsupported; 2-week expiry needs inhale top +8%, 3-4 weeks needs +10% or more to push the pierce rate below 15%, and at that point the return is about the same as not selling.

Transition point = the first bar in the inhale whose close is below the high of the bar 2 bars earlier; exhale = pullback or range, until the close crosses back above the inhale top. 'Historically reclaimed' = the share of this ticker's past exhales that reclaimed the inhale top within the same number of days, which is the pierce risk of placing the strike at the inhale top.
